50:["$","p",null,{"children":["The TCP client pings the server on its own every ",["$","code",null,{"children":"HeartbeatInterval"}]," while connected, so an idle session survives the server's heartbeat verification and consumer-group memberships and assignments stay fresh. The ping goes through the regular request path: a lost session is repaired by the same reconnect and auto-login any other request would trigger. 5e:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["The encryptor applies to ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"every message on the connection"}]," - topics mixing encrypted and plaintext messages are not supported."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":"You own the encryptor and must dispose it once the client is done with it."}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["With an encryptor configured, polling with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"autoCommit: true"}]," throws ",["$","code",null,{"children":"InvalidOperationException"}]," by default: the server commits the offset ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"before the client decrypts"}],", so a decryption failure would silently skip the whole batch.
